##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: household.h_hoh_test1_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons for whom Test 1 applies: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, except under a multiple support agreement"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: ["dependent.hoh_test1", {const: true}]}
  - id: household.h_hoh_test2_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons for whom Test 2 applies: a person who lived with you for more than half of the year in a home whose upkeep you paid over half of (a dependent other than the excluded kinds, or a qualifying child listed in items 2 to 4)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: ["dependent.hoh_test2", {const: true}]}
  - id: household.h_hoh_married
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married at the end of 2025 and not considered unmarried (you can't check the Head of household box unless you are unmarried or considered unmarried)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.ever_married", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.married_dec31", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.considered_unmarried", {const: false}]}
  - id: household.hoh_fails
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the \"Head of household\" box only if you are unmarried (or considered unmarried) and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies; this is true when that condition is not met"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.h_hoh_married", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["household.spouse_died_this_year", {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: ["household.h_hoh_test1_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: ["household.home_cost", {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: ["household.h_hoh_test2_count", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this interface. Read here as head of household status being unavailable. It is true in three cases. (1) The filer is married at year end and not considered unmarried. (2) The spouse died in 2025, so the filer is considered married for the whole year (Pub. 501). (3) Neither Test 1 nor Test 2 applies. For Test 2 the filer must have paid over half the cost of keeping up their own home (household.home_cost) and have at least one dependent entry with dependent.hoh_test2 true. Test 1 needs a dependent entry with dependent.hoh_test1 true; it concerns the parent's home, so it does not depend on home_cost. It is assumed, not verified, that the per-dependent fields dependent.hoh_test1 and dependent.hoh_test2 encode the IRS Test 1 and Test 2 conditions. When ever_married is false, married_dec31 is not needed; the all-test is false on that ground."
```

```

Second transcription's note: The pages do not define this interface. Read here as head of household status being unavailable. It is true in three cases. (1) The filer is married at year end and not considered unmarried. (2) The spouse died in 2025, so the filer is considered married for the whole year (Pub. 501). (3) Neither Test 1 nor Test 2 applies. For Test 2 the filer must have paid over half the cost of keeping up their own home (household.home_cost) and have at least one dependent entry with dependent.hoh_test2 true. Test 1 needs a dependent entry with dependent.hoh_test1 true; it concerns the parent's home, so it does not depend on home_cost. It is assumed, not verified, that the per-dependent fields dependent.hoh_test1 and dependent.hoh_test2 encode the IRS Test 1 and Test 2 conditions. When ever_married is false, married_dec31 is not needed; the all-test is false on that ground.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass bea3663eb74a
